# 1. Human decisions now resolved

## 1.1 Speed-to-Lead GitHub branch protection

**Decision:** Do **not** pay for a GitHub plan upgrade at this time.

The Speed-to-Lead repository remains private.

Until a future hosting/plan decision changes this:

```text
PR-first working agreement
CI/checks before merge
AI agents must not intentionally push directly to main
no force-push
no branch deletion
handoff records branch/PR/merge SHA
```

This is **process enforcement only**, not server-enforced GitHub branch protection.

Do not:

```text
make the repo public merely to obtain free branch protection
buy/upgrade GitHub solely for this temporary repository architecture
claim branch protection is technically enforced when it is not
```

Record this as temporary technical/process debt.

---

## 1.2 Jira live integration — AUTHORIZED

The human has authorized the Executive Agent / ACMS Jira integration to access everything available to the dedicated Startup Teams Jira account.

### Jira account identity

```text
site: startupteams.atlassian.net
account: startupteamscompany@gmail.com
scope: any Jira project/resource accessible to this account
```

### Secret handling

A Jira login secret was supplied by the human in the chat used to prepare this plan.

**Do not copy or reproduce that password in:**

```text
Git
Markdown
PR bodies
handoffs
logs
shell history
environment dumps
screenshots
tests
fixtures
```

For API integration, prefer an Atlassian API token / supported service credential rather than persisting the account password.

If the supplied login password cannot be used for the Jira REST API, create/use an Atlassian API token via the approved account workflow.

Store Jira secrets only in the approved CT122 secret/environment mechanism.

Never echo the secret during verification.

## 1.3 Jira authority and workflow policy

Jira remains the **human-facing planning layer**.

ACMS remains the **AI execution/orchestration layer**.

Canonical model:

```text
Human Jira issue
→ assigned to Startup Teams AI/Executive identity
→ Executive Agent reads goal/context
→ ACMS creates internal Work Items
→ workers execute
→ Executive Agent posts concise status/progress back to Jira
```

### Jira microtask policy

Do **not** create one Jira ticket per ACMS Work Item or worker task.

Invariant:

```text
Jira issue
≠
ACMS microtask
```

One Jira item may correspond to many internal ACMS Work Items.

### Jira comments

Comments/status updates should be **short**.

For software work, prefer:

```text
<one-line status heading>
PR: https://github.com/.../pull/123
```

If multiple PRs exist:

```text
Implementation ready for review.
PRs:
- https://github.com/.../pull/123
- https://github.com/.../pull/124
```

Do not duplicate detailed implementation summaries already present in GitHub PRs.

Use Jira for:

```text
high-level state
blockers
short progress
human review signal
links to PRs/artifacts
```

Use GitHub PRs for technical implementation detail.

---

## 1.4 Jira status-transition authority

The Executive Agent may transition Jira workflow state **only when**:

```text
the Jira issue is assigned to startupteamscompany@gmail.com
```

For issues assigned to anyone else:

```text
read/comment only
do not change workflow status
```

### Intended state behavior

When a Jira issue assigned to the AI/Executive account becomes approved/ready work:

```text
move to the project's appropriate "In Progress" equivalent
```

When the Executive Agent believes the measurable deliverable is complete:

```text
move to the project's appropriate "In Review" / review-equivalent state
```

Do **not** automatically move work to a final human-acceptance state such as:

```text
Done
Closed
Accepted
Released
```

unless a future explicit policy authorizes that transition.

Humans remain responsible for final review/acceptance.

### Workflow mapping

Do not hard-code status names globally.

Inspect the actual Jira workflow for each project and map semantic states:

```text
READY / APPROVED
IN_PROGRESS
IN_REVIEW
FINAL / DONE
```

If an unambiguous transition does not exist:

```text
leave status unchanged
post concise comment
raise Human Attention / configuration need
```

Create/provision a **dedicated least-privilege fine-grained GitHub credential** for CT122 economics ingestion.

Required scope should be read-only and limited to the Startup Teams repositories needed by the economics importer.

Minimum intended read permissions:

```text
pull requests
commits

```

Do not grant:

```text
contents write
PR write
issues write
administration
secrets
actions write
packages write

```

### Important platform constraint

If GitHub does not allow the token to be created programmatically using the currently available identity/tools:

1. prepare exact one-time human UI instructions;
2. request the human create the token;
3. have the human place it directly in CT122's approved secret/environment mechanism;
4. continue all other work without blocking the entire sprint.

Do not ask the human to paste the token into chat.

Use a dedicated environment name such as:

```text
ACMS_GITHUB_ECONOMICS_TOKEN
```

or the existing canonical secret variable if one already exists.

Do not overload a write-capable deployment token if a separate read-only credential is practical.

## 1.8 ADR-0012 — ACCEPTED

**ADR-0012 is accepted.**

Canonical completion architecture:

```text
Bridge/worker completion callback
    = primary immediate completion signal

ACMS reconciliation sweep
    = fallback / repair mechanism
```

Completion callback carries durable semantic data only:

```text
task/run ID
terminal status:
  SUCCEEDED
  FAILED
  CANCELLED
completed_at
result/handoff reference
usage/cost references when available
concise error summary when failed
```

Do not include:

```text
full transcript
token stream
verbose reasoning/context
```

### Idempotency and authority

Required behavior:

```text
duplicate callback
→ harmless / no duplicate close

late callback after reconciliation
→ harmless

unknown task ID
→ reject + audit

wrong agent/runtime identity
→ fail closed + audit

lost callback
→ reconciliation eventually repairs state
```

# 5. Phase C — implement ADR-0012 completion callback

## C1. Bridge → ACMS callback

Implement authenticated terminal-result delivery.

Prefer the established A2A/result path or a small versioned callback endpoint.

Do not introduce another messaging platform.

## C2. Identity verification

Callback must bind:

```text
ACMS agent ID
runtime ID
execution task ID
Bridge/A2A run ID
```

A runtime must not be able to close another runtime's task.

## C3. On terminal result

Atomically update:

```text
execution_tasks terminal state
ExecutionSession CLOSED
ended_at
result/handoff reference
final usage/cost correlation
semantic audit event
```

Attention behavior:

```text
SUCCEEDED → normally no Attention
FAILED → Attention based on existing severity policy
CANCELLED → semantic event, Attention only if context warrants
```

## C4. Retry/idempotency

Support safe callback retries.

A repeated success callback may return an idempotent success response.

## C5. Reconciliation fallback

Keep a sweep that checks nonterminal ACMS tasks against authoritative Bridge/runtime state.

Use reconciliation for:

```text
lost callback
temporary network partition
service restart
late callback
```

## C6. Live proof

Run one deterministic bounded task against VM124.

Success criteria:

```text
dispatch
→ execution session auto-opens
→ Hermes completes
→ callback arrives
→ task terminal
→ session CLOSED
→ cost link preserved
→ no manual close
```

Checkpoint.

# 6. Phase D — wire clone hygiene into the live provisioner

PR #57's checks become mandatory before a runtime reaches READY.

## D1. Provisioning gate

Validate:

```text

unique MAC

unique IP
Kea/DHCP/static inventory
no inherited static-netplan conflict
guest identity ↔ ARM metadata
Bridge identity ↔ ACMS agent
```

## D2. Fail closed

If any required uniqueness/identity check fails:

```text
do not mark READY
do not register Bridge as healthy
record durable provisioning failure
```

## D3. Retry

Retry must use newly validated identity/network allocation when the previous candidate conflicts.

## D4. Test

Use a disposable worker.

Prove the former `.203` duplicate-IP class is rejected before READY.

Do not modify VM906.

Checkpoint.

Never put secrets in release metadata.\n- Feature-specific smoke hooks via `ACMS_FEATURE_SMOKE` env (plan §11).\n\n## Live CT122 quirks (validated 2026-09-26)\n\n- Git checkout is at `/opt/acms/repo` (NOT `/opt/acms`); `.env` at\n  `/opt/acms/.env` (0600, key names only — never echo values).\n- **Compose v2 container names are `acms-<service>-1`** — bare\n  `docker exec acms-app` FAILS (\"No such container\"). Always\n  `docker compose -f deploy/compose.yaml --env-file /opt/acms/.env exec -T\n  <service>` (service-name agnostic). If a manual hop stage dies on a bare\n  name AFTER copying the maintenance conf, the host\n  `deploy/reverse-proxy/nginx.conf` is already switched —\n  `git checkout -- deploy/reverse-proxy/nginx.conf` BEFORE retrying, or the\n  retry double-applies maintenance content.\n- No `pg_dump`/`psql` on the CT host — run inside the postgres container:\n  `docker compose -f deploy/compose.yaml --env-file /opt/acms/.env exec -T\n  postgres pg_dump -U acms -Fc acms`.\n- No `curl` in the app image — probe with `docker exec acms-acms-app-1\n  python -c \"import urllib.request; ...\"` on 127.0.0.1:8000.\n- nginx allowlists 10.0.10.0/24 + 10.0.20.0/24 — validate via the VM's own IP\n  `https://10.0.20.122` with `curl -k` (self-signed; MARION has NO home.arpa\n  DNS — raw IPs everywhere).\n- `/version` returns `{version, git_sha, git_sha_short, build_time}` — the\n  git_sha identity check catches wrong-image states instantly. Run it after\n  ANY container recreation.\n- LLDAP 10.0.20.101:3890, base dc=example,dc=com; groups acms-admin/16\n  (jordatech), acms-workers/17, acms-observers/18.\n\n## Docker/Compose pitfalls (from the live drill — see references file)\n\n- **Single-file bind mounts pin the inode**: swapping the file on disk is\n  invisible to the container; HUP reload then serves stale config forever.\n  nginx now uses a directory mount (`./reverse-proxy:/etc/nginx/conf.d`) and\n  the maintenance template is named `*.conf.template` so the include glob\n  never loads it. Never re-introduce a single-file conf mount.\n- **`docker compose up -d <svc>` recreates depends_on services too** and can\n  bring the app back with the default image (`acms-app:local`). Pin\n  `ACMS_APP_IMAGE_TAG=<tag>` on EVERY compose up in tooling.\n- **Never command-substitute `compose build`** — its stdout is the build log\n  and pollutes captured variables (`invalid tag`). Send build output to\n  `>&2`; compute the tag, never capture it.\n- **pipefail + `grep -c` false positive**: `grep -c` exits 1 when the count\n  is 0; capture the count into a variable with `|| true`, compare numerically.\n- Recovery from stuck maintenance mode: `up -d --force-recreate\n  reverse-proxy`, then re-pin the app image, then verify via `/version`.\n\n## Bootstrap hop (chicken-and-egg first deploy)\n\nThe first deployment of the release tooling cannot use the tooling. Manual\nhop with identical guarantees: verified backup → stop app → checkout target\nSHA → build SHA-tagged image → migrate → start → validate → write release\nmetadata (mark `mode=manual-bootstrap`). Proven 2026-09-26 (~2 min window).\n\n## Verification hygiene (file-tool corruption lesson)\n\nGround truth for any file change is `git diff` / `py_compile` / `bash -n` —\nnever the tool result echo. Echoes can mislead in BOTH directions (phantom\nerrors and phantom success; this session had mid-write corruption injecting\ngarbage text). For large/critical writes: verify immediately after writing,\nand if corrupted, repair via a python rewrite with assertion pre-checks on\nknown anchor lines, then re-verify with git diff.\n\n## Pointers\n\n- `references/live-drill-2026-09-26.md` — bug-by-bug drill breakdown, exact\n  commands, recovery timeline.\n- `references/hermes-harness-live-tests-2026-09.md` — Hermes 0.17.0 live\n  control-mapping results (pause/resume unsupported, steer/interrupt verified),\n  scratch-profile setup recipe, bridge-to-ACMS wiring.\n- `references/hermes-worker-bringup.md` — the full worker-runtime bring-up recipe\n  (VM clone → Hermes venv install via qga → LLM gateway wiring incl. context caps →\n  api-server platform config shape → systemd unit → verification + traps) as\n  executed on acms-worker-001.\n- `references/ip-collision-10-0-20-203-case-2026-09-29.md` — the template-clone\n  impostor-IP case (symptoms, ARP diagnosis, VM108 renumber, generalization).\n- Repo docs: `deploy/README.md` (operator runbook), ADR-0009 + ADR-0010 (both\n  Accepted 2026-09-26/27), `docs/HARNESS_CONTROL_MAPPING.md`, `SPRINT.md`\n  (current slices), handoffs under `docs/handoffs/`.\n\n## Combined slice 3+4 state (deployed 2026-09-27, v0.6.0 @ 85f3c92)\n\n- **ADR-0009 Accepted** (amended: live evidence + explicit deployment-authority\n  policy); allocated in `create_work_item` / `assign_primary`.\n- State dimensions stay separate (plan §5): connectivity is ACMS-derived\n  (UNKNOWN/HEALTHY/STALE/UNREACHABLE from contact recency), `agent_running`\n  is harness-reported tri-state — the UI shows both as independent facts.\n- Heartbeat = complete snapshot (`acms-heartbeat-v1`), unknown fields rejected\n  by the Pydantic model; SQLite unit tests DON'T\n  enforce FKs so the whole suite passed while prod 500'd. Work-scoped events\n  now leave `agent_id` NULL. **Lesson: SQLite-with-FKs-off is a silent prod\n  gap class — when a unit DB is SQLite, grep for sentinel `\"\"` foreign-key\n  values before trusting green tests.** Candidate FUTURE_WORK: FK pragma in\n  the unit engine.\n- **C4 (PR #32):** `AGENT_RUNNING_WITHOUT_ASSIGNMENT` = informational, emitted\n  ONCE per state entry (scheduler in-memory dedupe dict, reset on state exit),\n  never per tick; severity `info`; NO auto-remediation (human direction:\n  never auto-stop). Migrations `0004_provisioning_requests`, `0005_prov_attempt`.\n- **State honesty (live-found bug):** the provision route MUST reflect the returned SM job state — SM job FAILED → request FAILED with the error, DONE → LIVE + runtime_id correlation via `list_runtimes`, else PROVISIONING. Local: `bash deploy/build-release.sh . dist` → tar.gz + sha256.\n   Build the artifact from the MAIN TIP, not your feature branch: create a\n   throwaway `git switch -c tmp-deploy-<sha> origin/main`, build from there,\n   then switch back (2026-09-29 pattern; keeps the artifact == merged main).\n2. Serve: `python3 -m http.server 8899 --bind 0.0.0.0` from `dist/`\n   (Hermes background terminal); kill it after (`pkill -f 'http.server 8899'`).\n   **Simpler than http.server: `scp` the tarball+sha directly to\n   `vm114:/tmp/`** (VM114 has sshd via the dedicated key) — used successfully\n   2026-09-29 for both releases.\n3. On VM114: `sha256sum -c <tarball>.sha256` → OK.\n4. Run the CURRENT deployment's `deploy-release.sh` (`/opt/llm-manager/current/deploy/deploy-release.sh <tarball>`)\n   via `setsid nohup ... > /tmp/deploy-<sha>.log 2>&1 < /dev/null &` (plain\n   nohup from a ssh 'bash -c' dies with the session; setsid survives).\n5. Poll `tail` of the log via ssh until `==> Release ACCEPTED (<full-sha>)`\n   + `healthcheck PASSED`. Note: `pgrep -f deploy-release` matches your OWN\n   ssh command line — filter with `pgrep -fl 'deploy-release.sh /tmp'` or\n   you'll read a phantom \"still running\".\n6. If `server_manager/` code changed: `systemctl restart server-manager-api`\n   (its own systemd unit; NOT restarted by the web-app transaction).\n7. Verify: `curl -sk https://10.0.20.108/healthz` reports the new git_sha.\n   Clean the tarball+sha out of `/tmp` after success.\n\n**Preflight checksum trap (cost a release cycle 2026-09-28):** the `.sha256`\nfile must sit NEXT TO the tarball AND be named `<tarball>.sha256`. The build\nnames the sha file after the CONTENT hash, not the tarball — preflight\n`sha256sum -c` then fails and the release aborts. Fix before transfer:\n`mv <content-hash>.sha256 <tarball>.sha256` (edit the single line to point at\nthe tarball filename if the manifest lists the content-hash name).\n\n## CI / merge discipline\n\n- 6 required checks on PRs: secret-scan, syntax, tests, deps, config, db\n  (+ integration-smoke). Never record nonconvergence without the wait.\n- DESIRED_DESTROYED is API-only; the reconciler NEVER destroys. PVE\n  unreachable → record `last_error`, no state flip on unknown ground truth.\n- Single-replica discipline (TDR-0009): one uvicorn worker; no leader election\n  yet. state-sync health vocabulary: PENDING|SYNCING|VERIFIED|STALE|FAILED.\n- Stale failed-provisioning rows: disposition is **SUPERSEDED** (never delete).\n  Admin endpoint marks each failed attempt row SUPERSEDED and links it to the\n  live runtime (5 ERROR rows → live VM124 runtime 56c1b849, 2026-09-28, PR\n  #50). Backoff 10/20/40/60s capped\n  (attempts vary). - **PROOF (06:07 UTC):** artifact from main tip → preflight PASS → candidate install → validation → migrations applied vs LOCAL staging DB (ledger v001) → atomic activate → ordered restarts (recovery LAST) → healthcheck 13/13 PASS → **Release ACCEPTED (2e2c3360c55d)**. Staging live on main tip.
- **The earlier e5fe98d FAILED transaction (05:21–05:31) was the pipeline working:** strict-old healthcheck failed → auto-rollback fired → rollback healthcheck also failed (old-script bug) → ledger recorded `rollback_degraded` → manual recovery → pipeline hardened. First real-world exercise of plan §14.
- **Lesson (chicken-and-egg):** deploy tooling fixes only reach staging THROUGH the tooling. When transaction scripts themselves change, unpack the new artifact to /tmp and run ITS deploy-release.sh against its own tarball — the artifact self-hosts its tooling.

[Subdirectory context discovered: work/acms-project-framework/AGENTS.md]
# AGENTS.md

Instructions for autonomous and semi-autonomous agents working in ACMS.

## Authority model

Humans set product intent, approve scope, approve significant decisions, define mandatory governance boundaries, and approve merges.

Agents may inspect the repository, plan, implement approved requirements, decompose approved scope into tasks, create tests, update affected documentation, create handoffs, add out-of-scope discoveries to `FUTURE_WORK.md`, draft ADRs/TDRs, and execute non-destructive work inside explicitly approved scope/budget/security/environment boundaries.

Agents must not silently add/remove product scope, promote future work, accept their own new significant ADR, merge their own feature work, exceed a budget, cross tenant/context boundaries, deploy to production, alter credentials/permissions, perform destructive infrastructure actions, or modify protected Organization/Product/Project/Feature context without required human authorization.

## Read before planning

For every meaningful change read:

1. `README.md`
2. `REQUIREMENTS.md`
3. `SPRINT.md`
4. `docs/BUSINESS_PROBLEMS.md`
5. `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`
6. relevant accepted/proposed ADRs
7. `FUTURE_WORK.md` when scope/follow-up work is involved
8. `INITIAL_IDEAS.md` when source provenance matters

## Requirement discipline

Implementation must trace to one or more `ACMS-REQ-###` entries. Before editing summarize the requirement IDs, acceptance criteria, files/components likely to change, assumptions, and validation.

If work is outside approved requirements, ask for human approval or record it in `FUTURE_WORK.md`.

## Future-work provenance

Every future-work item must identify one of:

- **Human-Directed**
- **Agent-Discovered**
- **Source-Derived**

Human-Directed does not mean approved; it remains future scope until promoted.

## Centralized governance, decentralized execution

ACMS controls approved assignment, scope, budget, context access, permissions, and governance. It must not become the synchronous permission service for every shell command/tool call/test/retry.

A worker agent may autonomously execute implementation details inside its approved envelope.

An agent may have:

- one primary ACMS work assignment; and
- zero or more registered background routines/cron jobs.

## Context authority

Protected context levels:

- Organization
- Product
- Project
- Feature

Agents may propose changes but may not modify these without human authorization or explicit delegated authority.

Agent, Task, and Live Session context may be modified within approved policy.

If approved scope appears wrong, execute it as far as reasonably possible, preserve state, create a handoff, and propose the alternate direction instead of silently changing scope.

## Handoffs

Meaningful work must produce/update a Markdown handoff recording:

1. assigned goal and requirement IDs;
2. what was attempted;
3. what actually changed;
4. what the agent believes the current state is;
5. validation and results;
6. blockers/assumptions/unresolved questions;
7. recommended next action.

## ADR policy

Human-explicit durable architecture decisions may be recorded as `Accepted`. Agent-originated significant decisions remain `Proposed` until human approval.

## Approval timeout policy

- Reversible in-scope decision: checkpoint/preserve state, use documented fallback, continue if safe.
- Consequential but deferable action: defer that action and continue unrelated work.
- Mandatory human gate: never infer approval from timeout; hold/deny the gated action and continue only unrelated authorized work.

## Security

- Never commit credentials/secrets/customer-sensitive data.
- External/customer agents are a separate trust domain.
- Internal Agent Bridge endpoints are private-network/VPN endpoints unless an accepted ADR says otherwise.
- Do not weaken authentication/authorization/audit to simplify implementation.

## Git workflow

The documentation bootstrap is human-authorized for direct import. After bootstrap, feature work must use a dedicated branch and PR.

Recommended branch: `type/ACMS-short-description`.

Recommended commit: `type(scope): short description (ACMS-REQ-###)` plus issue/ticket when available.

Every feature PR must list requirement IDs, acceptance criteria, validation, risks, ADR/TDR changes, future work, and reviewer focus. Agents do not self-merge.

[Subdirectory context discovered: work/llm-manager-project-framework/AGENTS.md]
# AGENTS.md — LLM Manager

These instructions apply to AI agents modifying the LLM Manager repository or MARION-IA-USA LLM Manager deployment.

## 1. Read before changing anything

Read, in order:

1. `README.md`
2. `REQUIREMENTS.md`
3. `FUTURE_WORK.md`
4. `SPRINT.md`
5. `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`
6. `docs/IMPLEMENTATION_STATUS.md`
7. `docs/OPERATIONS_AND_USAGE.md`
8. relevant ADRs/TDRs

Approved requirements define scope. Future work is not authorization to implement.

## 2. Service boundary

LLM Manager owns inference/runtime infrastructure concerns. ACMS owns work/project/context orchestration.

Do not move project requirements, Kanban/task state, persistent transcript history, human steering governance, or general ACMS context management into LLM Manager merely because an integration needs them.

## 3. Production safety rules

- Treat MARION-IA-USA as production infrastructure.
- Never replace a known-working model/runtime before capturing its exact rollback configuration.
- Put deployments into explicit maintenance before authorized manual work.
- Recovery has historically had more than one trigger; verify both desired service state and desired power/VM state before stopping a VM.
- Do not assume a stopped model stays stopped until recovery state is verified.
- Never start two VMs that share the same passthrough GPU devices.
- Avoid repeated rapid GPU-VM stop/start cycles on MIAM-00111; a known PCIe/AER/riser condition has previously required a host reboot to recover.
- Preserve custom/patched vLLM environments. Do not `pip install -U vllm` into a known-good runtime.
- Experimental model stacks belong in versioned paths/containers/VMs.

## 4. Configuration and model truth

The live registry and verified runtime state are authoritative for current deployments.

Do not infer that an alias is local merely from its name. Verify provider/runtime/model identity.

When documenting a model deployment, capture:

- actual artifact/revision/checksum;
- runtime/image/commit;
- quantization;
- host/VM/site;
- context;
- TP/PP/DP/EP and relevant runtime flags;
- RAM/VRAM/hardware;
- route/alias;
- health and benchmark evidence.

## 5. History and rollback

Deployment history must make engineering work reproducible.

- `Load Configuration` is non-destructive.
- `Restore & Serve` is a separate confirmed operation.
- Failed experiments may be retained but hidden by default.
- Do not delete the last known-good revision merely because a newer model works.

## 6. Benchmark discipline

A performance number without deployment context is misleading.

Link benchmarks to exact deployment revisions and record at least:

- TTFT;
- output tokens/sec per request/agent;
- aggregate output tokens/sec;
- concurrency;
- prompt/output token counts;
- tested context;
- RAM/VRAM;
- host/hardware/runtime;
- errors;
- representative Hermes/tool-use status when relevant.

When comparing models, prefer same-node tests. When comparing nodes, use the same model/configuration first so hardware effects are visible.

## 7. Secrets and data

Never commit:

- `.env` files containing secrets;
- API keys/tokens;
- passwords;
- SSH/private keys;
- production database row dumps;
- customer-sensitive data;
- raw logs containing credentials.

Sanitize configuration into `.example` files. Run a secret scan before commit. If a secret appears in a tool transcript or repository candidate, stop and surface it for rotation/removal.

## 8. Git workflow

- Work on a dedicated branch.
- Branch format: `type/ticket-short-description`.
- Commit format: `type(scope): short description (#issue-id)`.
- Do not invent an issue ID; create/use an approved issue according to repository policy.
- Keep capture/bootstrapping changes reviewable.
- A human must approve before merge.

## 9. ADR policy

If a human explicitly made a significant architecture decision, record it as an ADR and surface it in the PR.

If the agent thinks a significant new decision is required, draft `Status: Proposed` and stop for human approval before treating it as accepted.

## 10. TDR policy

Use a TDR for intentional shortcuts, patched runtimes, manual recovery procedures, missing automation, known hardware/software limitations, or other accepted debt.

## 11. Validation

Run the exact project validation commands once captured/documented. At minimum for a Python service, consider syntax/import checks, tests, config parsing, migration/schema verification, and API smoke tests in a safe environment.

Report `Pass`, `Fail`, or `Not run` accurately. Never infer a passing test.

## 12. Stop and ask for human judgment when

- requirements conflict;
- an ACMS-facing contract would be invented or materially changed;
- a destructive production action is required outside the approved maintenance scope;
- credentials/access are required and unavailable;
- a security boundary changes;
- production tests fail unexpectedly;
- a new persistent model policy would be made without approved authority;
- the diff expands beyond the approved capture/documentation scope.

## 13. Handoff standard

At completion provide:

1. requirements addressed;
2. source/code captured and source locations;
3. files excluded and why;
4. changes made;
5. validation commands/results;
6. production impact (if any);
7. ADR/TDR status;
8. future-work items discovered;
9. known risks/unresolved questions;
10. exact branch, commit, PR, and recommended next action.

{"content": "1|\"\"\"Automatic ExecutionSession lifecycle around dispatch (2026-09-29 plan §8 / Phase F).\n2|\n3|Target chain:\n4|    Work → Assignment → budget check → ExecutionSession → Bridge/A2A task →\n5|    telemetry/cost → result → session close → handoff/checkpoint\n6|\n7|Requirements:\n8|    - duplicate dispatch does NOT duplicate the session (idempotent open by a2a_task_id)\n9|    - hard-budget reject does NOT create a fake RUNNING/OPEN session\n10|    - Bridge failure does not leave a zombie OPEN session\n11|    - success closes correctly\n12|    - A2A/task/model/provider/cost correlation preserved\n13|    - failed runs retain their cost (close with status CLOSED; cost fields untouched)\n14|\"\"\"\n15|\n16|from __future__ import annotations\n17|\n18|from sqlalchemy import select\n19|from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession\n20|\n21|from .memory_models import ExecutionSessionRecord\n22|from .telemetry_service import add_event, _now\n23|\n24|\n25|async def _find_by_a2a(db: AsyncSession, a2a_task_id: str | None) -> ExecutionSessionRecord | None:\n26|    if not a2a_task_id:\n27|        return None\n28|    row = await db.scalar(\n29|        select(ExecutionSessionRecord)\n30|        .where(ExecutionSessionRecord.a2a_task_id == str(a2a_task_id))\n31|        .order_by(ExecutionSessionRecord.started_at.desc())\n32|        .limit(1)\n33|    )\n34|    return row\n35|\n36|\n37|async def open_session_for_dispatch(\n38|    db: AsyncSession, *, agent_id: str, work_item_id: str,\n39|    assignment_id: str | None, a2a_task_id: str | None,\n40|    harness_session_id: str | None = None,\n41|) -> ExecutionSessionRecord:\n42|    \"\"\"Idempotently open (or return the existing) session for this A2A run.\"\"\"\n43|    existing = await _find_by_a2a(db, a2a_task_id)\n44|    if existing is not None and existing.status == \"OPEN\":\n45|        return existing  # duplicate dispatch: never duplicate the session\n46|    rec = ExecutionSessionRecord(\n47|        session_id=ExecutionSessionRecord.new_id(), agent_id=agent_id,\n48|        work_item_id=work_item_id, assignment_id=assignment_id,\n49|        a2a_task_id=str(a2a_task_id) if a2a_task_id else None,\n50|        harness_session_id=harness_session_id,\n51|        status=\"OPEN\", started_at=_now(),\n52|    )\n53|    db.add(rec)\n54|    await add_event(db, event_type=\"EXECUTION_SESSION_OPENED\", actor_source=\"dispatch_lifecycle\",\n55|                    agent_id=agent_id, work_key=None,\n56|                    summary=f\"Execution session auto-opened for dispatch (a2a={str(a2a_task_id)[:24]})\",\n57|                    metadata={\"session_id\": rec.session_id, \"work_item_id\": work_item_id,\n58|                              \"a2a_task_id\": str(a2a_task_id) if a2a_task_id else None})\n59|    return rec\n60|\n61|\n62|async def fail_session_for_dispatch(\n63|    db: AsyncSession, *, session: ExecutionSessionRecord | None, reason: str,\n64|) -> None:\n65|    \"\"\"Bridge failure: never leave a zombie OPEN session.\"\"\"\n66|    if session is None or session.status != \"OPEN\":\n67|        return\n68|    session.status = \"CLOSED\"\n69|    session.ended_at = _now()\n70|    await add_event(db, event_type=\"EXECUTION_SESSION_CLOSED\", actor_source=\"dispatch_lifecycle\",\n71|                    agent_id=session.agent_id, work_key=None,\n72|                    summary=f\"Execution session closed after dispatch failure: {reason[:120]}\",\n73|                    metadata={\"session_id\": session.session_id, \"failure\": True})\n74|    # cost fields intentionally untouched: failed runs retain their cost (REQ-058)\n75|\n76|\n77|async def close_session(\n78|    db: AsyncSession, *, session_id: str, outcome: str = \"COMPLETED\",\n79|) -> ExecutionSessionRecord | None:\n80|    \"\"\"Explicit close (bridge telemetry / dispatcher completion path).\"\"\"\n81|    rec = await db.get(ExecutionSessionRecord, session_id)\n82|    if rec is None or rec.status == \"CLOSED\":\n83|        return rec\n84|    rec.status = \"CLOSED\"\n85|    rec.ended_at = _now()\n86|    await add_event(db, event_type=\"EXECUTION_SESSION_CLOSED\", actor_source=\"dispatch_lifecycle\",\n87|                    agent_id=rec.agent_id, work_key=None,\n88|                    summary=f\"Execution session closed ({outcome})\",\n89|                    metadata={\"session_id\": session_id, \"outcome\": outcome})\n90|    return rec\n91|\n92|\n93|async def reconcile_zombie_sessions(db: AsyncSession, *, max_age_hours: float = 24.0) -> int:\n94|    \"\"\"Close OPEN sessions whose A2A run is gone/terminal and older than max_age.\n95|\n96|    Conservative: only closes sessions with no recent checkpoint and an A2A id\n97|    (those are bridge-proven runs). Returns count closed.\n98|    \"\"\"\n99|    cutoff = _now()\n100|    # Normalize tz-awareness: SQLite returns naive datetimes; PG returns aware.\n101|    if cutoff.tzinfo is None:\n102|        cutoff = cutoff.replace(tzinfo=None)\n103|\n104|    rows = (await db.scalars(\n105|        select(ExecutionSessionRecord)\n106|        .where(ExecutionSessionRecord.status == \"OPEN\")\n107|        .where(ExecutionSessionRecord.a2a_task_id.isnot(None))\n108|        .where(ExecutionSessionRecord.started_at < cutoff)\n109|    )).all()\n110|    closed = 0\n111|    for r in rows:\n112|        started = r.started_at\n113|        if started.tzinfo is not None and cutoff.tzinfo is None:\n114|            started = started.replace(tzinfo=None)\n115|        elif started.tzinfo is None and cutoff.tzinfo is not None:\n116|            started = started.replace(tzinfo=cutoff.tzinfo)\n117|        age_h = (cutoff - started).total_seconds() / 3600.0\n118|        if age_h >= max_age_hours:\n119|            r.status = \"CLOSED\"\n120|            r.ended_at = _now()\n121|            await add_event(db, event_type=\"EXECUTION_SESSION_CLOSED\", actor_source=\"dispatch_lifecycle\",\n122|                            agent_id=r.agent_id, work_key=None,\n123|                            summary=f\"Zombie session reconciled closed (age {age_h:.1f}h)\",\n124|                            metadata={\"session_id\": r.session_id, \"zombie\": True})\n125|            closed += 1\n126|    return closed\n127|", "total_lines": 126, "file_size": 5600, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

{"content": "1|\"\"\"Agent Bridge for Hermes (combined slice 3+4 plan §15; ADR-0010; REQ-031/032).\n2|\n3|Translates ACMS bridge capabilities (status / send_work / steer / pause /\n4|resume / interrupt / cancel / request_handoff / set_session_title) to the\n5|**live-tested** Hermes api-server surface (docs/HARNESS_CONTROL_MAPPING.md).\n6|\n7|Capability honesty (plan §12/§13): `pause`/`resume` are NOT supported by\n8|Hermes 0.17.0 (verified live: 404) and are advertised unsupported — never\n9|faked with interrupt.\n10|\n11|Bridge identity/credentials come from ``acms_bridge_targets`` config (env:\n12|``ACMS_BRIDGE_TARGETS`` = JSON list of {agent_id, base_url, api_key}); the\n13|bridge runs where Hermes runs (VM906) or anywhere with network access to the\n14|private gateway — ACMS core never imports this module in tests that don't\n15|exercise it (plan §15: Hermes internals stay out of ACMS domain logic).\n16|\"\"\"\n17|from __future__ import annotations\n18|\n19|import json\n20|import urllib.error\n21|import urllib.request\n22|from dataclasses import dataclass\n23|\n24|from .telemetry_models import HeartbeatPayload, HeartbeatIdentity, HeartbeatSession\n25|\n26|# Capabilities proven live against Hermes 0.17.0 (2026-09-26/27; see\n27|# docs/HARNESS_CONTROL_MAPPING.md for the test log).\n28|HERMES_SUPPORTED_CAPABILITIES = (\n29|    \"status\", \"send_work\", \"steer\", \"interrupt\", \"cancel\",\n30|    \"request_handoff\", \"set_session_title\",\n31|)\n32|\n33|\n34|class BridgeError(Exception):\n35|    \"\"\"Bridge transport/protocol failure (maps to reconciliation failure).\"\"\"\n36|\n37|\n38|def _post_json(url: str, key: str, body: dict | None = None, method: str = \"POST\",\n39|               headers: dict | None = None, timeout: int = 20) -> dict:\n40|    data = json.dumps(body or {}).encode()\n41|    req = urllib.request.Request(url, data=data, method=method)\n42|    req.add_header(\"Authorization\", f\"Bearer {key}\")\n43|    req.add_header(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n44|    for k, v in (headers or {}).items():\n45|        req.add_header(k, v)\n46|    try:\n47|        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as resp:\n48|            return json.loads(resp.read().decode())\n49|    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:\n50|        detail = e.read().decode(errors=\"replace\")[:300]\n51|        raise BridgeError(f\"{e.code} {detail}\") from None\n52|    except (urllib.error.URLError, TimeoutError, OSError) as e:\n53|        raise BridgeError(str(e)) from None\n54|\n55|\n56|def _get_json(base_url: str, key: str, path: str, timeout: int = 10) -> dict:\n57|    req = urllib.request.Request(f\"{base_url}{path}\")\n58|    req.add_header(\"Authorization\", f\"Bearer {key}\")\n59|    try:\n60|        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as resp:\n61|            return json.loads(resp.read().decode())\n62|    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:\n63|        raise BridgeError(f\"{e.code}\") from None\n64|    except (urllib.error.URLError, TimeoutError, OSError) as e:\n65|        raise BridgeError(str(e)) from None\n66|\n67|\n68|@dataclass\n69|class BridgeTarget:\n70|    agent_id: str\n71|    base_url: str\n72|    api_key: str\n73|    harness: str = \"hermes\"\n74|\n75|\n76|def load_targets() -> list[BridgeTarget]:\n77|    from .settings import get_settings\n78|\n79|    raw = get_settings().bridge_targets_json\n80|    targets = []\n81|    for item in json.loads(raw) if raw else []:\n82|        targets.append(BridgeTarget(\n83|            agent_id=item[\"agent_id\"],\n84|            base_url=item[\"base_url\"].rstrip(\"/\"),\n85|            api_key=item[\"api_key\"],\n86|            harness=item.get(\"harness\", \"hermes\"),\n87|        ))\n88|    return targets\n89|\n90|\n91|def get_bridge_for_agent(agent_id: str):\n92|    \"\"\"Factory used by the reconciliation scheduler (lazy import kept here).\n93|\n94|    Phase G: resolution is ARM-authoritative (bridge_discovery); the manual\n95|    ACMS_BRIDGE_TARGETS_JSON list remains as fallback. Fail-closed either way.\n96|    \"\"\"\n97|    from .bridge_discovery import DiscoveryError, discover_bridge\n98|\n99|    try:\n100|        d = discover_bridge(agent_id)\n101|        return HermesBridge(BridgeTarget(\n102|            agent_id=d.agent_id, base_url=d.base_url,\n103|            api_key=d.api_key, harness=d.harness,\n104|        ))\n105|    except DiscoveryError as e:\n106|        raise BridgeError(str(e)) from None\n107|\n108|\n109|class HermesBridge:\n110|    \"\"\"Tested Hermes api-server mapping. Every method here corresponds to a\n111|    row in docs/HARNESS_CONTROL_MAPPING.md.\"\"\"\n112|\n113|    def __init__(self, target: BridgeTarget):\n114|        self._t = target\n115|\n116|    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- status\n117|\n118|    def fetch_status(self) -> HeartbeatPayload:\n119|        \"\"\"Full status snapshot via the tested mapping (reconciliation path).\n120|\n121|        Raises BridgeError when unreachable — the scheduler maps that to\n122|        UNREACHABLE per ADR-0010.\"\"\"\n123|        sessions = self._get(\"/api/sessions\")\n124|        rows = sessions.get(\"data\") or sessions.get(\"sessions\") or []\n125|        if not rows:\n126|            raise BridgeError(\"no sessions reported\")\n127|        # Most recently active session = the agent's current work context.\n128|        rows.sort(key=lambda s: s.get(\"last_active\") or 0, reverse=True)\n129|        cur = rows[0]\n130|        sess = self._get(f\"/api/sessions/{cur['id']}\").get(\"session\", {})\n131|        return HeartbeatPayload(\n132|            schema_version=\"acms-heartbeat-v1\",\n133|            identity=HeartbeatIdentity(\n134|                acms_agent_id=self._t.agent_id,\n135|                bridge_id=\"hermes-api-server\",\n136|                harness=\"hermes\",\n137|            ),\n138|            session=HeartbeatSession(\n139|                session_id=str(sess.get(\"id\") or cur.get(\"id\") or \"\"),\n140|                title=sess.get(\"title\"),\n141|                agent_running=False,  # sessions API has no live running flag; reconciliation is contact-only\n142|            ),\n143|            platforms={\"connected\": [\"api_server\"]},\n144|        )\n145|\n146|    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- controls\n147|\n148|    def send_work(self, *, work_key: str, assignment_key: str, instruction: str,\n149|                  session_id: str | None = None) -> dict:\n150|        \"\"\"Dispatch work. Uses run submission (new session) or session-bound\n151|        chat (existing session); includes ACMS keys in metadata per REQ-052.\"\"\"\n152|        meta = {\"acms_work_key\": work_key, \"acms_assignment_key\": assignment_key,\n153|                \"source\": \"acms\"}\n154|        if session_id:\n155|            return _post_json(f\"{self._t.base_url}/api/sessions/{session_id}/chat\",\n156|                              self._t.api_key,\n157|                              {\"message\": instruction},\n158|                              headers={\"X-Hermes-Session-Id\": session_id})\n159|        return _post_json(f\"{self._t.base_url}/v1/runs\", self._t.api_key, {\n160|            \"input\": [{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": instruction}],\n161|            \"metadata\": meta,\n162|            \"title\": f\"[{work_key}] ACMS dispatched work\",\n163|        })\n164|\n165|    def steer(self, session_id: str, message: str) -> dict:\n166|        return _post_json(f\"{self._t.base_url}/api/sessions/{session_id}/chat\",\n167|                          self._t.api_key,\n168|                          {\"message\": message},\n169|                          headers={\"X-Hermes-Session-Id\": session_id})\n170|\n171|    def interrupt(self, run_id: str) -> dict:\n172|        return _post_json(f\"{self._t.base_url}/v1/runs/{run_id}/stop\", self._t.api_key)\n173|\n174|    # `cancel` uses the same tested terminal endpoint as interrupt (A2A-task\n175|    # level); the Hermes session survives — new execution task to continue.\n176|    cancel = interrupt\n177|\n178|    def set_session_title(self, session_id: str, title: str) -> dict:\n179|        return _post_json(f\"{self._t.base_url}/api/sessions/{session_id}\",\n180|                          self._t.api_key, {\"title\": title}, method=\"PATCH\")\n181|\n182|    def request_handoff(self, session_id: str, work_key: str) -> dict:\n183|        \"\"\"Plan §10: structured Markdown handoff request (via send_work).\"\"\"\n184|        instruction = (\n185|            f\"Produce a structured Markdown handoff for [{work_key}]: what you \"\n186|            \"believe you are doing, current goal, actions completed, \"\n187|            \"artifacts/files changed, blockers, and next intended action. \"\n188|            \"Use 'Observed:' vs 'Interpretation:' conventions.\"\n189|        )\n190|        return self.send_work(work_key=work_key, assignment_key=\"\",\n191|                              instruction=instruction, session_id=session_id)\n192|\n193|    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- helpers\n194|\n195|    def _get(self, path: str) -> dict:\n196|        return _get_json(self._t.base_url, self._t.api_key, path)\n197|", "total_lines": 196, "file_size": 8215, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

### [2026-09-29T14:27:10.985283+00:00] tool · tool: read_file

{"content": "1|\"\"\"A2A work dispatch — the single authoritative path for starting NEW cloud\n2|execution of a Work Item on a worker agent (REV2 Phase C, REQ-059 gate).\n3|\n4|Design rules from the plan:\n5|- ONE dispatch point. UI/API layers call this service; budget checks are never\n6|  duplicated in several callers.\n7|- Gate BEFORE new cloud execution: budget execution-check verdict decides.\n8|  - below hard limit  → dispatch\n9|  - soft exceeded     → dispatch + retain the warning in the event trail\n10|  - hard exceeded     → no NEW dispatch; durable event + Attention item\n11|                        (EXECUTION_REJECTED); in-flight work untouched\n12|  - hard + override   → dispatch + audited override trail already exists\n13|- Unknown cost stays UNKNOWN — never a fabricated zero (the budget engine\n14|  itself never blocks on unknown cost; the dispatch path preserves the fact).\n15|- Idempotency: a client-supplied idempotency key (or a stable hash of the\n16|  work/assignment pair) maps to ONE execution task; retries return the\n17|  original task instead of dispatching twice.\n18|- Correlation recorded on the task + event: Work ID/key, assignment, bridge\n19|  run id, budget-check verdict (audit trail), external A2A task id.\n20|\"\"\"\n21|from __future__ import annotations\n22|\n23|import hashlib\n24|from typing import Any\n25|\n26|from sqlalchemy import select\n27|from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession\n28|\n29|from . import budget_service, work_service\n30|from .session_lifecycle import fail_session_for_dispatch, open_session_for_dispatch\n31|from .bridge import BridgeError, get_bridge_for_agent\n32|from .telemetry_service import add_event\n33|from .work_models import ExecutionTaskCreate, ExecutionTaskRecord, WorkItemRecord\n34|\n35|\n36|class DispatchError(Exception):\n37|    \"\"\"Machine-readable dispatch failure (message = stable error code).\"\"\"\n38|\n39|    def __init__(self, code: str, http_status: int = 409, detail: dict | None = None):\n40|        super().__init__(code)\n41|        self.code = code\n42|        self.http_status = http_status\n43|        self.detail = detail or {}\n44|\n45|\n46|def _idempotency_key(work_item_id: str, assignment_id: str | None,\n47|                     client_key: str | None) -> str:\n48|    \"\"\"Stable dedupe key. Client key wins; else the open assignment; else work.\"\"\"\n49|    basis = client_key or assignment_id or work_item_id\n50|    return hashlib.sha256(f\"acms-dispatch:{basis}\".encode()).hexdigest()[:32]\n51|\n52|\n53|async def _find_existing_task(db: AsyncSession, dedupe_key: str) -> Any | None:\n54|    \"\"\"The most recent task created from this dedupe key.\n55|\n56|    Tasks carry the dedupe key as external_task_id ``disp-<key>`` — the\n57|    ExecutionTaskRecord therefore doubles as the idempotency ledger without a\n58|    new table.\n59|    \"\"\"\n60|    rows = (await db.execute(\n61|        select(ExecutionTaskRecord)\n62|        .where(ExecutionTaskRecord.external_task_id == f\"disp-{dedupe_key}\")\n63|        .order_by(ExecutionTaskRecord.started_at.desc())\n64|        .limit(1)\n65|    )).scalars().all()\n66|    return rows[0] if rows else None\n67|\n68|\n69|async def dispatch_work(db: AsyncSession, *, work_item_id: str,\n70|                        instruction: str, agent_id: str | None = None,\n71|                        idempotency_key: str | None = None,\n72|                        session_id: str | None = None,\n73|                        actor: str = \"api\") -> dict[str, Any]:\n74|    \"\"\"Dispatch NEW cloud execution for a Work Item — the ONLY path that does.\n75|\n76|    Returns a dict:\n77|      dispatched: bool          — False when the gate blocked or dedupe hit\n78|      reason: str               — dispatched | hard_budget_exceeded | duplicate\n79|      task_id / external_task_id — correlation ids\n80|      budget_check: dict        — the verdict used for the gate (audit trail)\n81|    Raises DispatchError for structural failures (unknown work/agent/bridge).\n82|    \"\"\"\n83|    # ---- 1. resolve the work item (+ open assignment) ----------------------\n84|    work = await db.get(WorkItemRecord, work_item_id)\n85|    if work is None:\n86|        raise DispatchError(\"unknown-work-item\", 404)\n87|    assignments = await work_service.list_assignments(db, work_item_id=work_item_id)\n88|    active = next((a for a in assignments if a.status == \"ACTIVE\"), None)\n89|    if agent_id is None and active is None:\n90|        raise DispatchError(\"no-active-assignment\", 409)\n91|    target_agent = agent_id or active.agent_id\n92|    if active is not None and agent_id is not None and agent_id != active.agent_id:\n93|        raise DispatchError(\"agent-mismatch\", 409,\n94|                            {\"active_assignment_agent\": active.agent_id})\n95|\n96|    # ---- 2. idempotency: a retry must never double-dispatch ----------------\n97|    dedupe_key = _idempotency_key(work_item_id, active.assignment_id if active else None,\n98|                                  idempotency_key)\n99|    existing = await _find_existing_task(db, dedupe_key)\n100|    if existing is not None and existing.status == \"RUNNING\":\n101|        return {\"dispatched\": False, \"reason\": \"duplicate\",\n102|                \"task_id\": existing.task_id, \"external_task_id\": None,\n103|                \"budget_check\": None}\n104|\n105|    # ---- 3. budget gate (REQ-059) BEFORE any cloud execution ---------------\n106|    verdict = await budget_service.check_new_execution_allowed(db, work_item_id)\n107|    if not verdict.get(\"allowed\", True):\n108|        # hard exceeded without override → durable rejection event (Attention\n109|        # picks it up from the log; no separate mutable state) + hold dispatch.\n110|        await add_event(\n111|            db,\n112|            event_type=\"EXECUTION_REJECTED\",\n113|            actor_source=\"dispatch\",\n114|            agent_id=target_agent,\n115|            work_key=work.work_key,\n116|            assignment_key=active.assignment_key if active else None,\n117|            summary=(\"NEW cloud execution blocked: hard budget exceeded on \"\n118|                     f\"{work.work_key or work_item_id[:8]} \"\n119|                     f\"(state={verdict.get('budget_state')}; \"\n120|                     f\"override={'active' if (verdict.get('override') or {}).get('active') else 'none'})\")[:512],\n121|            metadata={\"budget_check\": verdict, \"dispatch_attempt\": True,\n122|                      \"work_item_id\": work_item_id, \"dedupe_key\": dedupe_key},\n123|        )\n124|        await db.commit()\n125|        return {\"dispatched\": False, \"reason\": \"hard_budget_exceeded\",\n126|                \"task_id\": None, \"external_task_id\": None,\n127|                \"budget_check\": verdict}\n128|\n129|    # ---- 4. resolve the bridge target --------------------------------------\n130|    bridge = get_bridge_for_agent(target_agent)\n131|    if bridge is None:\n132|        raise DispatchError(\"no-bridge-target\", 503, {\"agent_id\": target_agent})\n133|\n134|    # ---- 5. dispatch through the bridge (new A2A run) ----------------------\n135|    session_for_run = None  # set on success; failure path closes it if set\n136|    try:\n137|        result = bridge.send_work(\n138|            work_key=work.work_key or work_item_id,\n139|            assignment_key=(active.assignment_key if active else \"\") or \"\",\n140|            instruction=instruction,\n141|            session_id=session_id,\n142|        )\n143|    except BridgeError as e:\n144|        # dispatch itself failed — record it; budget NOT consumed (ACMS did not\n145|        # create a session), retry after the error is resolved.\n146|        await add_event(\n147|            db,\n148|            event_type=\"EXECUTION_FAILED\",\n149|            actor_source=\"dispatch\",\n150|            agent_id=target_agent,\n151|            work_key=work.work_key,\n152|            assignment_key=active.assignment_key if active else None,\n153|            summary=f\"A2A dispatch failed: {str(e)[:180]}\",\n154|            metadata={\"dedupe_key\": dedupe_key, \"budget_check\": verdict,\n155|                      \"work_item_id\": work_item_id},\n156|        )\n157|        await fail_session_for_dispatch(db, session=session_for_run, reason=str(e))\n158|        await db.commit()\n159|        raise DispatchError(\"bridge-error\", 502, {\"detail\": str(e)[:300]}) from None\n160|\n161|    run_id = (result or {}).get(\"run_id\") or (result or {}).get(\"id\")\n162|\n163|    # Phase F (plan §8): the dispatch automatically owns an ExecutionSession.\n164|    # Idempotent on the A2A run id — a duplicate dispatch never duplicates it.\n165|    session_for_run = await open_session_for_dispatch(\n166|        db, agent_id=str(target_agent), work_item_id=work_item_id,\n167|        assignment_id=(active.assignment_id if active else None),\n168|        a2a_task_id=str(run_id) if run_id else None,\n169|        harness_session_id=(result or {}).get(\"session_id\"),\n170|    )\n171|\n172|    # ---- 6. record the execution task + correlation ------------------------\n173|    task, err = await work_service.create_execution_task(db, ExecutionTaskCreate(\n174|        work_item_id=work_item_id,\n175|        agent_id=target_agent,\n176|        external_task_id=f\"disp-{dedupe_key}\",\n177|    ))\n178|    if task is None:\n179|        raise DispatchError(err or \"task-record-failed\", 500)\n180|\n181|    # ---- 7. durable audit trail --------------------------------------------\n182|    await add_event(\n183|        db,\n184|        event_type=\"EXECUTION_DISPATCHED\",\n185|        actor_source=\"dispatch\",\n186|        agent_id=target_agent,\n187|        work_key=work.work_key,\n188|        assignment_key=active.assignment_key if active else None,\n189|        a2a_task_id=str(run_id) if run_id else None,\n190|        summary=(f\"Dispatched [{work.work_key or work_item_id[:8]}] to {str(target_agent)[:8]}\"\n191|                 + (f\" (soft exceeded: {verdict.get('budget_state')})\"\n192|                    if verdict.get(\"budget_state\") == \"SOFT_EXCEEDED\" else \"\"))[:512],\n193|        metadata={\"run_id\": run_id, \"budget_check\": verdict,\n194|                  \"dedupe_key\": dedupe_key, \"task_id\": task.task_id,\n195|                  \"assignment_id\": active.assignment_id if active else None,\n196|                  \"work_item_id\": work_item_id, \"actor\": actor},\n197|    )\n198|    await db.commit()\n199|    return {\"dispatched\": True, \"reason\": verdict.get(\"reason\", \"dispatched\"),\n200|            \"task_id\": task.task_id, \"external_task_id\": str(run_id) if run_id else None,\n201|            \"budget_check\": verdict}\n202|", "total_lines": 201, "file_size": 9743, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

{"content": "1|\"\"\"Background scheduling for live telemetry (combined slice 3+4; ADR-0010; plan §16).\n2|\n3|Lightweight in-process loop — no Celery/K8s/event bus. Runs inside the FastAPI\n4|lifespan; every DB operation gets its own session so the loop never holds a\n5|request-scoped session. Time is injected (``now_fn``) so tests never wait real\n6|minutes/hours (plan §20).\n7|\n8|Jobs:\n9|- stale evaluation (every tick): derive UNKNOWN/HEALTHY/STALE/UNREACHABLE\n10|- 1-hour stale reconciliation: actively request a full status snapshot\n11|- 24 h fleet reconciliation (staggered tolerance documented in ADR-0010)\n12|- telemetry sampling every ACMS_TELEMETRY_SAMPLE_SECONDS + on material change\n13|\n14|Reconciliation authority (plan §9): correct operational telemetry, preserve\n15|history, log discrepancy events; never touch approved scope/assignment.\n16|Multi-replica note: a single ACMS instance is assumed; replicas would need\n17|leader/lock coordination (documented in ADR-0010 + FUTURE_WORK).\n18|\"\"\"\n19|from __future__ import annotations\n20|\n21|import asyncio\n22|import json\n23|import logging\n24|from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\n25|\n26|from sqlalchemy import select\n27|from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession\n28|\n29|from .db import SessionLocal\n30|from .telemetry_models import (\n31|    AgentEventRecord,\n32|    AgentStatusCurrentRecord,\n33|    AgentTelemetrySampleRecord,\n34|    CONNECTIVITY_HEALTHY,\n35|    CONNECTIVITY_STALE,\n36|    CONNECTIVITY_UNREACHABLE,\n37|    CONTACT_SOURCE_RECONCILIATION,\n38|)\n39|from .telemetry_service import TelemetryService, _new_id, add_event, _now\n40|\n41|logger = logging.getLogger(\"acms.telemetry.scheduler\")\n42|\n43|\n44|class TelemetryScheduler:\n45|    def __init__(self, session_factory=None, now_fn=None):\n46|        self._session_factory = session_factory or SessionLocal\n47|        self._now_fn = now_fn or _now\n48|        self._task: asyncio.Task | None = None\n49|        self._last_fleet_reconcile: datetime | None = None\n50|        self._last_sample: dict[str, datetime] = {}\n51|        # C4 state-entry dedupe: AGENT_RUNNING_WITHOUT_ASSIGNMENT is emitted once\n52|        # per state entry (never per tick). Key = agent_id, value = currently in state.\n53|        self._running_unassigned: dict[str, bool] = {}\n54|\n55|    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ loop\n56|\n57|    def start(self) -> None:\n58|        if self._task is None or self._task.done():\n59|            self._task = asyncio.create_task(self._run(), name=\"acms-telemetry-scheduler\")\n60|    async def stop(self) -> None:\n61|        if self._task is not None:\n62|            self._task.cancel()\n63|            try:\n64|                await self._task\n65|            except asyncio.CancelledError:\n66|                pass\n67|            self._task = None\n68|\n69|    async def _run(self) -> None:\n70|        from .settings import get_settings\n71|\n72|        while True:\n73|            try:\n74|                s = get_settings()\n75|                await self.tick(s)\n76|            except asyncio.CancelledError:\n77|                raise\n78|            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — scheduler must survive transient DB issues\n79|                logger.exception(\"telemetry scheduler tick failed\")\n80|            await asyncio.sleep(5)\n81|    async def tick(self, s) -> None:\n82|        \"\"\"One scheduler pass. Public so tests can drive time deterministically.\"\"\"\n83|        now_dt = self._now_fn()\n84|        async with self._session_factory() as db:\n85|            statuses = await TelemetryService.list_status(db)\n86|            for status in statuses:\n87|                await self._evaluate_agent(db, s, status, now_dt)\n88|            await self._maybe_fleet_reconcile(db, s, now_dt)\n89|\n90|    # ------------------------------------------------------- per-agent logic\n91|\n92|    async def _evaluate_agent(self, db: AsyncSession, s, status: AgentStatusCurrentRecord, now_dt: datetime) -> None:\n93|        agent_id = status.agent_id\n94|        if status.last_contact_at is None:\n95|            return  # never contacted: stays UNKNOWN\n96|\n97|        stale_after = timedelta(seconds=s.heartbeat_stale_seconds)\n98|        reconcile_after = timedelta(seconds=s.stale_reconcile_seconds)\n99|        lc = status.last_contact_at\n100|        if lc.tzinfo is None:\n101|            from datetime import timezone as _tz\n102|            lc = lc.replace(tzinfo=_tz.utc)\n103|        delta = now_dt - lc\n104|\n105|        new_connectivity = status.connectivity\n106|        if delta <= stale_after:\n107|            new_connectivity = CONNECTIVITY_HEALTHY\n108|        elif delta >= reconcile_after:\n109|            ok = await self._attempt_reconciliation(db, s, status, now_dt)\n110|            new_connectivity = CONNECTIVITY_HEALTHY if ok else CONNECTIVITY_UNREACHABLE\n111|        else:\n112|            new_connectivity = CONNECTIVITY_STALE\n113|\n114|        if new_connectivity != status.connectivity:\n115|            prev = status.connectivity\n116|            status.connectivity = new_connectivity\n117|            if new_connectivity == CONNECTIVITY_STALE and status.stale_since is None:\n118|                status.stale_since = now_dt\n119|            elif new_connectivity == CONNECTIVITY_HEALTHY:\n120|                status.stale_since = None\n121|            status.updated_at = now_dt\n122|            await add_event(\n123|                db,\n124|                event_type=\"CONNECTIVITY_CHANGED\", actor_source=\"scheduler\", agent_id=agent_id,\n125|                summary=f\"Connectivity {prev} -> {new_connectivity} after {int(delta.total_seconds())}s without contact\",\n126|            )\n127|        await db.commit()\n128|\n129|        # C4: RUNNING without a primary ACMS assignment = informational state.\n130|        # Human direction 2026-09-29: do NOT automatically stop the runtime.\n131|        # Emit once per state entry; auto-remediation is deliberately absent.\n132|        await self._check_running_unassigned(db, status, now_dt)\n133|\n134|        # periodic telemetry sample (plan §8)\n135|        last = self._last_sample.get(agent_id)\n136|        if last is None or (now_dt - last).total_seconds() >= s.telemetry_sample_seconds:\n137|            await self._write_sample(db, status, now_dt)\n138|            self._last_sample[agent_id] = now_dt\n139|\n140|    async def _check_running_unassigned(self, db: AsyncSession, status: AgentStatusCurrentRecord,\n141|                                         now_dt: datetime) -> None:\n142|        \"\"\"Informational warning when the harness reports the agent RUNNING but\n143|        ACMS holds no ACTIVE primary assignment (Phase C4). No auto-remediation:\n144|        the runtime keeps running; humans see the Attention item.\"\"\"\n145|        from sqlalchemy import select\n146|        from .work_models import AssignmentRecord\n147|\n148|        running = (status.agent_running or \"\").lower() == \"true\"\n149|        in_state = self._running_unassigned.get(status.agent_id, False)\n150|        if not running:\n151|            if in_state:  # leaving the state: reset dedupe so a future entry re-emits\n152|                self._running_unassigned[status.agent_id] = False\n153|            return\n154|        active = (await db.execute(\n155|            select(AssignmentRecord).where(\n156|                AssignmentRecord.agent_id == status.agent_id,\n157|                AssignmentRecord.status == \"ACTIVE\",\n158|            ).limit(1)\n159|        )).scalars().first()\n160|        if active is not None:\n161|            if in_state:\n162|                self._running_unassigned[status.agent_id] = False\n163|            return\n164|        if in_state:\n165|            return  # already announced this entry — never per-tick spam\n166|        self._running_unassigned[status.agent_id] = True\n167|        await add_event(\n168|            db,\n169|            event_type=\"AGENT_RUNNING_WITHOUT_ASSIGNMENT\",\n170|            actor_source=\"scheduler\", agent_id=status.agent_id,\n171|            summary=(\"Agent RUNNING with no active primary ACMS assignment — \"\n172|                     \"informational; no automatic remediation\"),\n173|            metadata={\"detection\": \"scheduler\", \"policy\": \"observe-only\"},\n174|        )\n175|        await db.commit()\n176|\n177|    async def _attempt_reconciliation(self, db: AsyncSession, s, status: AgentStatusCurrentRecord, now_dt: datetime) -> bool:\n178|        \"\"\"Request a full status snapshot from the bridge (1 h stale path).\n179|\n180|        Bridge transport lands in Phase 4; until then reconciliation of an\n181|        agent without a live bridge fails (→ UNREACHABLE), which is the honest\n182|        outcome: we cannot reach it.\"\"\"\n183|        await add_event(db, event_type=\"RECONCILIATION_STARTED\",\n184|            actor_source=\"scheduler\", agent_id=status.agent_id,\n185|            summary=f\"Stale >= {s.stale_reconcile_seconds}s - requesting status snapshot\")\n186|        await db.commit()\n187|        try:\n188|            from .bridge import get_bridge_for_agent  # Phase 4 module\n189|\n190|            bridge = get_bridge_for_agent(status.agent_id)\n191|            snapshot = await bridge.fetch_status()\n192|            await TelemetryService.ingest_heartbeat(db, status.agent_id, snapshot)\n193|            await add_event(db, event_type=\"RECONCILIATION_SUCCEEDED\",\n194|                actor_source=\"reconciliation\", agent_id=status.agent_id,\n195|                summary=\"Reconciliation snapshot ingested; connectivity restored\")\n196|            await db.commit()\n197|            return True\n198|        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — no bridge / transport failure = unreachable\n199|            await add_event(db, event_type=\"RECONCILIATION_FAILED\",\n200|                actor_source=\"reconciliation\", agent_id=status.agent_id,\n201|                summary=f\"Reconciliation failed: {type(exc).__name__}\",\n202|                metadata={\"error\": str(exc)[:300]})\n203|            await db.commit()\n204|            return False\n205|\n206|    async def _maybe_fleet_reconcile(self, db: AsyncSession, s, now_dt: datetime) -> None:\n207|        if self._last_fleet_reconcile is None:\n208|            self._last_fleet_reconcile = now_dt\n209|            return\n210|        if (now_dt - self._last_fleet_reconcile).total_seconds() < s.fleet_reconcile_seconds:\n211|            return\n212|        self._last_fleet_reconcile = now_dt\n213|        logger.info(\"fleet reconciliation tick at %s\", now_dt.isoformat())\n214|        # Phase 4: per-agent reconciliation attempts for agents with bridges.\n215|        # Management scope is never touched here (ADR-0010).\n216|\n217|    async def _write_sample(self, db: AsyncSession, status: AgentStatusCurrentRecord, now_dt: datetime) -> None:\n218|        db.add(AgentTelemetrySampleRecord(\n219|            sample_id=_new_id(), agent_id=status.agent_id, sampled_at=now_dt,\n220|            agent_running=status.agent_running, connectivity=status.connectivity,\n221|            model_id=status.model_id,\n222|            context_used_tokens=status.context_used_tokens,\n223|            context_max_tokens=status.context_max_tokens,\n224|            context_utilization_percent=status.context_utilization_percent,\n225|            context_warning=status.context_warning,\n226|            session_id=status.session_id, session_title=status.session_title,\n227|            session_total_tokens=status.session_total_tokens,\n228|            cumulative_api_tokens=status.cumulative_api_tokens,\n229|        ))\n230|        await db.commit()\n231|", "total_lines": 230, "file_size": 10680, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}
